Consequently, the bound grows at most linearly, rather than exponentially, with the final time. We further derive spatial error estimates and an error decomposition that separates the effects of spatial discretization, temporal discretization, and proper orthogonal decomposition truncation. The reduced formulation replaces the large full-order systems with low-dimensional problems, enabling efficient long-time simulations. Numerical experiments verify the predicted convergence behavior, assess different inner products for constructing the reduced basis, and demonstrate accurate recovery of transient oscillations and viscoelastic relaxation with substantial computational savings, even after accounting for snapshot generation and basis construction.

</details>


### [117] [A linear mass-lumped finite element method for the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation: unconditional energy dissipation and length preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26687)
*Qiumei Huang,Binghong Li,Xiaoli Li,Cheng Wang,Jiang Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高度非线性Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）方程的线性、无条件能量耗散且质量集中（mass-lumped）的有限元方法，通过投影策略和混合有限元-有限差分框架同时保持节点单位长度约束与能量稳定性，并建立了最优阶误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLG方程具有非凸点态约束|m|=1，在标准有限元离散中同时保持该约束与无条件能量稳定性一直是挑战性问题。需要设计一种既能精确保持单位长度约束，又能保证无条件能量耗散的高效数值方法。

Method: 提出统一混合有限元-有限差分框架。在方案构造中，利用弱形式和mass-lumped有限元的节点结构，结合合适的插值算子及受有限差分启发的节点级长度保持机制，得到线性格式。该框架同时用于误差分析，结合有限元的弱形式和拟均匀网格结构，以及基于插值的节点有限差分技术来控制强非线性阻尼项。

Result: 得到了线性且节点级保长度的格式，满足离散能量耗散定律，建立了最优阶误差估计。数值实验（包括经典blow-up模拟）验证了预测的精度、能量耗散性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法系统性地整合了有限元的几何灵活性与有限差的点态约束保持特性，为约束耗散系统的保结构离散化设计提供了通用策略。

Abstract: We develop a linear, unconditionally energy-dissipative, mass-lumped finite element method for the highly nonlinear Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert (LLG) equation on quasi-uniform triangular meshes. The method is built on a projection strategy for enforcing the nonconvex pointwise constraint $|\mathbf{m}| = 1$, whose simultaneous preservation with unconditional energy stability remains challenging for standard finite element discretizations. The key innovation is a unified hybrid finite element-finite difference framework that underlies both the design and the analysis of the proposed method. In the scheme construction, we exploit the weak formulation and nodal structure of mass-lumped finite element method, while incorporating suitable interpolation operators and a node-wise length-preserving mechanism inspired by finite difference discretizations. This combination yields a linear scheme that preserves the node-wise unit-length constraint and satisfies a discrete energy dissipation law. The same hybrid framework also plays a central role in the error analysis, where the weak formulation and quasi-uniform mesh structure of finite elements are combined with interpolation-based and nodewise finite difference method to control the strongly nonlinear damping term and to establish an optimal-order error estimate. More importantly, the proposed method provides a unified framework that systematically integrates the geometric flexibility of finite element method with the pointwise constraint-preserving property of finite difference method, and thus offers a general strategy for designing and analyzing structure-preserving discretizations of constrained dissipative systems. Numerical experiments, including a classical blow-up simulation, confirm the predicted accuracy, energy dissipation, and robustness of the method.

</details>


### [118] [A Discontinuous Galerkin discretization for the sea ice dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26810)
*Emma Lagracie,Thomas Richter*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究Hibler粘塑性海冰模型的全间断Galerkin（DG）空间离散方法，并理论证明改进的弹粘塑性（mEVP）格式作为伪时间迭代求解器的收敛性，为海冰动力学模拟中线性运动特征（LKFs）的精确表征提供高精度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰动力学是气候模型的重要组成部分，但其数值模拟因非线性冰流变学和外部物理强迫的相互作用而具有挑战性。海冰呈现的线性运动特征（LKFs）——即与冰间水道开启或压力脊形成相关的窄带强烈变形——其数量、定位和结构对空间分辨率和速度场离散化高度敏感。现有离散化方法在准确表征这些特征方面存在局限，需要更鲁棒且高精度的数值方案。

Method: 采用全间断Galerkin（DG）方法对Hibler粘塑性海冰模型的所有变量（包括速度场）进行空间离散。DG元素能够表示间断且具有高阶局部多项式逼近能力，适合解析复杂冰变形特征并对网格诱导数值伪影具有鲁棒性。同时，对改进的弹粘塑性（mEVP）格式作为粘塑性（VP）动量方程的伪时间迭代求解器进行理论收敛性分析，证明底层连续伪时间动力系统收敛到粘塑性形式极限。

Result: 通过一个既定的海冰动力学基准测试评估了全DG方法，并将其获得的海冰变形结果与最先进的离散化方法进行了比较。此外，从理论上证明了mEVP格式作为Hibler VP模型迭代求解器的收敛性，为mEVP的实际应用提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 全DG方法能够有效表征海冰复杂的变形特征（如LKFs），并对网格诱导数值伪影具有鲁棒性；mEVP格式的收敛性得到了理论证明，从而为Hibler粘塑性海冰模型的数值求解提供了坚实的理论支撑。

Abstract: Sea ice dynamics plays a crucial role in the Earth's climate system, making it an important component of weather and climate prediction models. Its numerical simulation remains challenging, however, as it exhibits complex mechanical behaviors due to a nonlinear ice rheology and interactions with external physical forcings. In particular, sea ice presents linear kinematic features (LKFs), i.e., narrow bands of intense deformation associated with processes such as lead opening or pressure-ridge formation. Accurately representing these features is necessary as they affect thermodynamics and ocean-atmosphere exchange. Yet their number, localization, and structure are highly sensitive to spatial resolution and to the chosen discretization of the sea ice velocity. In this work, we investigate the spatial discretization of Hibler's viscous plastic sea-ice model using a fully discontinuous Galerkin (DG) representation of all variables, including the velocity field. The ability of DG elements to represent discontinuities while having high order local polynomial approximation makes them well suited for resolving the complex ice deformation features, and insuring robustness towards mesh-induced numerical artefacts. We assess the fully DG method using an established sea ice dynamics benchmark and compare the obtained sea ice deformation with state-of-the-art discretizations. Additionally, we study the theoretical convergence of the modified Elastic-Viscous-Plastic (mEVP) formulation as a pseudo-time iterative solver for the viscous-plastic (VP) momentum equations. We prove the convergence of the underlying continuous pseudo-time dynamical system towards the viscous plastic formal limit, thereby providing a theoretical foundation for the use of mEVP as an iterative solver for Hibler's VP model.

</details>


### [119] [A fast solver for many-particle elastic scattering in layered media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26875)
*Jinrui Zhang,Yixiao He,Jun lai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速求解器，用于处理多层介质中多个粒子对时谐弹性波的散射问题，支持Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件，结合Sommerfeld积分、边界积分公式、散射矩阵、多重散射理论及快速多极子方法，在二维和三维空间中实现高效精确求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类问题广泛存在于复合材料优化、无损检测和地下成像等应用中，但由于粒子间和层状界面的强多重散射相互作用，计算极具挑战性，现有方法难以兼顾精度和效率。

Method: 该方法通过Sommerfeld积分表示分层介质贡献，并与适定的边界积分公式耦合以描述粒子散射问题；采用高阶积分方程离散化和散射矩阵处理任意形状粒子；利用多重散射理论高效描述粒子间相互作用；为进一步降低大规模粒子系统的计算成本，使用快速多极子方法加速多重散射计算；算法同时在二维和三维空间中开发。

Result: 针对刚性粒子和无牵引粒子的数值实验验证了公式的准确性，并展示了该方法在直接散射模拟和逆散射应用中的灵活性。

Conclusion: 所提出的多粒子弹性散射快速求解器在精度和效率上均表现良好，适用于二维和三维问题，且能灵活应对直接散射与逆散射等多种应用场景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fast solver for time-harmonic elastic scattering by multiple particles embedded in layered media, with either Dirichlet or Neumann boundary conditions imposed on the particle surfaces. Such problems arise in many important applications, including composite material optimization, nondestructive testing, and subsurface imaging. They are computationally challenging because of strong multiple scattering interactions among the particles and the layered interface. The proposed method represents the layered medium contribution through Sommerfeld integrals and couples this representation with a well-posed boundary integral formulation for the particle scattering problem. High-order integral equation discretization and scattering matrix are used to handle particles of general shape, while multiple scattering theory provides an efficient description of particle interactions. To reduce the cost for large particle systems, the resulting multiple scattering computation is further accelerated by the fast multipole method. The main formulation is developed in both two and three dimensions. Numerical experiments for rigid and traction-free particles validate the accuracy of the formulation, and demonstrate its flexibility in both direct scattering simulations and inverse scattering applications.

</details>


### [120] [Spectral Convergence of the Multipole Expansion Method for Acoustic Scattering in Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26884)
*Jinrui Zhang,Jun Lai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了三维时谐声学多重散射中多极展开法（MEM）的谱收敛性。利用对角预条件单层公式，将截断系统在球谐能量空间中进行分析，通过将相互作用截断分解为目标侧和源侧高次部分并分别采用投影Green核表示和平移球波族的逐次估计，基于Parseval恒等式和球谐加法定理建立了通用收敛分析框架，并通过首次传递分析获得了更优估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多极展开法（MEM）是快速多极法（FMM）等快速算法的理论基础，但因其涉及无数波反射，三维情形下的收敛性此前尚未被证明。缺乏严格收敛性分析限制了该类快速算法的可靠性验证与进一步推广。

Method: 采用对角预条件单层公式，将N次截断系统置于自然的球谐能量空间中分析。将相互作用截断分解为目标侧与源侧两部分：目标侧用投影Green核表示，源侧用平移球波族的逐次估计；利用Parseval恒等式与球谐加法定理完成证明，并通过首次传递分析获得更sharp的估计。

Result: 证明了MEM对三维中有限个分离良好球体时谐声散射问题的谱收敛性，得到收敛因子并揭示其几何与物理来源。通过首次传递分析获得更优的收敛估计，数值实验验证了预测的谱衰减与几何收敛因子。

Conclusion: 该工作为一大类多重散射快速算法的收敛性分析奠定了基础，填补了MEM三维收敛性理论研究的空白。

Abstract: Multiple scattering is a fundamental wave interaction phenomenon in acoustics and electromagnetics. The multipole expansion method (MEM) is the basis of many fast algorithms, such as the fast multipole method (FMM), for such problems. However, due to the infinitely many wave reflections involved, its convergence in three dimensions remains unexplored. In this paper, we prove spectral convergence of the MEM for time-harmonic acoustic scattering by finitely many well-separated spheres in three dimensions. Using a diagonally preconditioned single-layer formulation, we analyze the degree-$N$ truncated system in a natural spherical harmonic energy space. We split the interaction truncation into target-side and source-side high-degree parts and choose a different representation for each: a projected Green-kernel representation for the former and degree-wise estimates of a translated spherical wave family for the latter. The resulting argument, based on Parseval's identity and the spherical harmonic addition theorem, provides a general framework for the convergence analysis of MEM and reveals the geometric and physical origins of the convergence factors. We also obtain a sharper estimate through the first-transfer analysis. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted spectral decay and the geometric convergence factor. This paves the way for the convergence analysis of a large class of fast algorithms for multiple scattering.

</details>


### [121] [Waveguiding in systems of high contrast resonators: Theory and fast computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26906)
*Habib Ammari,Bowen Li,Borui Miao,Jiayu Qiu,Lara Vrabac*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高对比度谐振器系统中超出亚波长区间的、在非零内部Neumann频率附近的导波模式。作者引入了一个无限维、频率依赖的电容算符（通过将外部Helmholtz Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射压缩到内部共振Neumann特征空间的迹上获得），证明了连续问题向该离散有效算符的范数-预解收敛性，并利用Combes-Thomas论证建立了电容系数的指数衰减，从而允许指数精确的截断和局部计算。通过引入适当量级（√δ）的复吸收和Hermitian对称化，可移除稳定性假设，得到对缺陷本征频率O(δ²)精度的快速计算方法。数值实验验证了方法对偶极和四极谐振以及直/弯曲波导的准确性和指数局域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高对比度介质研究主要集中在亚波长（低频）区域，而对于非零内部Neumann频率附近的导波模式（超出亚波长机制）缺乏系统的有效离散化理论。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立高对比度谐振器系统在更高频率下的严格数学框架和高效计算方法。

Method: 通过压缩外部Helmholtz Dirichlet-to-Neumann(DtN)映射到内部共振Neumann特征空间的迹，构造无限维频率依赖电容算符；利用范数-预解收敛证明连续到离散的有效逼近；通过Combes-Thomas论证建立系数指数衰减；引入复吸收和Hermitian对称化以去掉稳定性假设；结合截断半径和吸收参数的量级分析，发展快速计算方法。

Result: 证明了连续问题到离散有效算符的范数-预解收敛，推导出紧凑缺陷频率和线缺陷能带的线性渐近公式；建立了电容系数的指数衰减，展示局部问题的指数收敛逼近；数值实验验证了对偶极和四极谐振以及直/弯曲波导的准确性和指数局域性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了超出亚波长范围的高对比度谐振器导波模式的严格离散化理论，证明了有效算符的指数局域性，并提供了一种以O(δ²)精度、吸收参数O(√δ)、截断半径O(|logδ|)的高效计算方法，为高对比度波导系统提供了可靠且计算可行的工具。

Abstract: In this work, we study guided modes in systems of high-contrast resonators near nonzero interior Neumann frequencies, beyond the subwavelength regime. In the regular exterior regime, where the exterior Dirichlet problem is well-posed at the reference wavenumber, we introduce an infinite-dimensional frequency-dependent capacitance operator obtained by compressing the exterior Helmholtz Dirichlet-to-Neumann map to the traces of the interior resonant Neumann eigenspaces. We prove the norm-resolvent convergence of the continuous problem to this discrete effective operator as the contrast $δ\to0$, and derive first-order asymptotic formulas for compact-defect frequencies and line-defect band functions. We then establish exponential off-diagonal decay of the capacitance coefficients by a Combes--Thomas argument, yielding an exponentially accurate truncation of the discrete operator, and show that its retained coefficients can be computed from local Helmholtz problems. At the physical frequency, this local approximation converges exponentially under a uniform stability assumption for the growing finite-cluster problems. The stability assumption can be removed by introducing a vanishing complex absorption together with a Hermitian symmetrization. In particular, an absorption parameter of order $\sqrtδ$, together with interaction truncation and patch radii of order $|\logδ|$, suffices to preserve the $O(δ^2)$ accuracy of the first-order high-contrast expansion of the defect eigenfrequencies, yielding a fast computational method. Numerical experiments for dipole and quadrupole resonances illustrate the accuracy, exponential locality, and applicability of the discrete model to straight and bent waveguides generated by material or geometric detuning.

</details>


### [122] [Stiff Norm Estimates for Variable-Stepsize Peer Two-Step Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26913)
*Jens Lang,Bernhard A. Schmitt*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为变步长对角隐式L(α)-稳定Peer两步方法在刚性半线性系统上建立了传播算子的严格范数估计，通过构造具有网格无关左特征向量的方法类，实现了超越经典Runge-Kutta阶段阶障碍的高阶段阶方法（可达阶5），并给出三个基准问题的比较数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性问题的高效数值求解需要兼具高精度、强稳定性和避免阶降的格式。Peer两步方法结合了Runge-Kutta和多步方法的优点，但在变步长情形下缺乏严格的稳定性理论。本文旨在为变步长对角隐式L(α)-稳定Peer方法建立严格的范数估计，填补该理论空白。

Method: 论文聚焦于传播矩阵具有网格无关左特征向量的一类Peer方法，利用该性质为整个系数族构造合适的矩阵范数。在此基础上，证明这类方法可达到阶段阶q=s以及超收敛阶s+1（s为阶段数），超越了经典不可约s级对角隐式Runge-Kutta方法的阶段阶障碍q≤2。文中构造了直到阶5的具体方法，并对3、4、5阶三种新Peer方法及四种同阶或更高阶SDIRK方法进行了数值对比。

Result: 建立了变网格上刚性半线性问题的一致稳定性界；构造了阶段阶q=s、超收敛阶s+1的L(α)-稳定Peer方法（最高至阶5），显著超越经典DIRK方法的阶段阶限制；通过三个经典刚性基准问题的数值实验验证了方法的有效性，与多个阶的SDIRK方法进行了系统比较。

Conclusion: 该理论为变步长Peer方法在刚性半线性问题上的稳定性提供了严格保证，并为进一步应用于最优控制和相关边值问题奠定了基础。高阶段阶的优势在刚性微分方程中能有效缓解阶降现象。

Abstract: Peer two-step methods are attractive for the numerical solution of stiff initial value problems because they combine favorable features of Runge-Kutta and multi-step methods, including high-order accuracy, strong stability properties, and the avoidance of order reduction. This paper develops rigorous norm estimates for the propagation operators of variable-stepsize diagonally-implicit $L(α)$-stable Peer methods applied to stiff semi-linear systems. The analysis relies on a class of Peer methods whose propagation matrices possess a grid-independent left eigenvector, enabling the construction of suitable matrix norms for entire coefficient families. We show that this class contains $L(α)$-stable methods of stage order $q=s$ and super-convergence of order $s+1$ with $s$ stages, exceeding the classical stage-order barrier $q\le 2$ of irreducible $s$-stage diagonally-implicit Runge-Kutta methods, and construct such methods up to order 5. Achieving high stage order is one of the principal advantages of Peer methods, especially for stiff differential equations where it helps mitigate order reduction. The resulting theory provides uniform stability bounds for stiff semi-linear problems on variable grids and establishes a foundation for further applications to optimal control and related boundary value problems. Comparative numerical results are presented for three classical stiff benchmark problems, including three novel Peer two-step methods of order 3, 4, 5, and four singly diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta methods with a first explicit step of stage order 2 and order 3, 4, 5, and 6.

</details>


### [123] [QH-GEM: Quantum-Hydrodynamic Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27216)
*Harbir Antil,Alex Kaltenbach,Sarswati Shah*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自由粒子薛定谔方程Madelung表述的确定性、物理约束生成框架。随机性仅来自初始采样，后续生成完全确定，通过求解PDE约束的相位识别问题实现终端时刻分布匹配，并给出了解析条件与误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成模型依赖随机微分方程或独立的参数化时变速度场，缺乏物理约束且计算成本高。本文旨在利用量子力学中Madelung流体力学表述，构建一个确定性生成框架，使随机性仅体现在初始采样阶段，从而简化生成过程并赋予其物理可解释性。

Method: 基于自由粒子薛定谔方程的Madelung表述，将Born概率密度与可控初始相位函数耦合，通过Bohm量子势建立联系；将终端分布匹配建模为PDE约束的相位识别问题，推导Hamiltonian与Fisher信息结构；对高斯波包给出解析解与精确可达性条件；建立特征流与一阶传输映射的O(T^2)误差界；并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 对各向同性高斯波包，获得封闭动力学与二次初始相位函数精确可达各向同性高斯目标的充要条件及采样映射；对光滑给定初始速度场，证明特征流以O(T^2)误差（一致范数与1、2-Wasserstein距离）逼近一阶传输映射；数值实验验证了全离散前向求解器及非对称双峰目标的PDE约束相位识别。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种物理约束的确定性生成范式，理论上保证了采样分布随时间沿特征流精确演变，兼具可解释性与效率，为生成建模提供了新的确定性途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a deterministic, physically constrained generative framework based on the Madelung formulation of the free-particle Schrödinger equation. A reference Born probability density and a controllable initial phase function serve as initial data for the free Madelung system, which couples the Born probability density and phase function through the Bohm quantum potential, while the phase function determines the hydrodynamic velocity field. Provided that the Born probability density remains positive and the hydrodynamic velocity field generates a unique global characteristic flow, samples drawn from the reference density and transported along the characteristic flow are distributed according to the evolving Born probability density at every time.
  As a consequence, randomness enters only through the initial sampling; the subsequent generation is deterministic and involves neither stochastic dynamics nor an independently parameterized time-dependent velocity field. We formulate terminal-time distribution matching as a PDE-constrained phase-identification problem and derive the underlying Hamiltonian and Fisher-information structure. For isotropic Gaussian wave packets, we obtain explicit dynamics and a necessary and sufficient condition for exact reachability of isotropic Gaussian targets by quadratic initial phase functions, together with the corresponding sampling map. For a smooth prescribed potential initial velocity field, we further establish that the characteristic flow approximates the associated first-order transport map with an O(T^2) error, both uniformly and in the 1- and 2-Wasserstein distances. A numerical Gaussian benchmark validates the fully discrete forward solver, while full-grid PDE-constrained phase identification is demonstrated for asymmetric bimodal targets.

</details>


### [124] [Scale-uniform inverse inequalities for scaled kernel spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26945)
*Davoud Mirzaei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了尺度一致的反不等式（scale-uniform inverse inequalities），用于由缩放正定核生成的函数空间。核的母空间为Sobolev空间时，在尺度相关Sobolev空间中建立反估计，并在有界域上得到以L²为较弱范数的标准Sobolev范数尺度一致反不等式；进一步在全空间上建立了更一般的尺度一致Bernstein不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在核近似方法的稳定性与收敛性分析中，反不等式是重要工具。然而在多尺度环境下，试验空间随核尺度δ变化，固定核的反估计不足以保证常数在δ→0时保持可控。因此需要尺度一致的反不等式。

Method: 首先在尺度相关的Sobolev空间中建立反估计，进而推广到有界域上的标准Sobolev范数尺度一致反不等式；然后在全空间上建立更一般的尺度一致Bernstein不等式。证明直接利用核空间中函数的Fourier表示，结合低频-高频分解与分离指数多项式的框架估计。

Result: 得到的估计仅需中心点的分离距离不超过核尺度的固定倍数，允许核尺度比分离距离下降得更慢，这与多尺度细化场景相关。更一般的Bernstein不等式覆盖了广泛的强弱Sobolev指标组合，且避免了分别比较缩放与标准Sobolev范数所产生的额外尺度因子。

Conclusion: 本文建立了多尺度核函数空间中的尺度一致反不等式与Bernstein不等式，为核近似方法在多尺度细化框架下的稳定性与收敛性分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Inverse inequalities are an important tool in the stability and convergence analysis of kernel approximation methods. In a multiscale setting, however, the trial space changes with the kernel scale $δ$, and inverse estimates for a fixed kernel are not sufficient. The constants must remain controlled as $δ\to0$. In this paper, we study scale-uniform inverse inequalities for spaces generated by scaled positive definite kernels whose native spaces are Sobolev spaces. We first establish inverse estimates in scale-dependent Sobolev spaces. On bounded domains, this leads to a scale-uniform inverse inequality in standard Sobolev norms with $L^2$ as the weaker norm. The estimate requires only that the separation distance of the centers be bounded above by a fixed multiple of the kernel scale. This allows the kernel scale to decrease more slowly than the separation distance, as is relevant in multiscale refinement. We then establish more general scale-uniform Bernstein inequalities on the whole space. Our result covers a broad range of weaker and stronger Sobolev indices under the same relation between the separation distance and the kernel scale. The proof works directly with the Fourier representation of functions in the kernel space and combines a low-high frequency decomposition with frame estimates for separated exponential polynomials. This avoids the additional scale factors that arise from separate comparisons of scaled and standard Sobolev norms.

</details>


### [125] [A quantum-assisted framework for PDE-based Bayesian inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27028)
*Dong An,Yinan Li,Pucheng Tang,Yunfeng Xiong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量-经典混合框架用于贝叶斯偏微分方程反演问题，通过归一化量子态损失函数和Hadamard测试实现仅用有限量子态副本即可提取经典信息，在1D/2D线性对流扩散方程和1D非线性受迫粘性Burgers方程上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子PDE求解器主要专注于制备量子态解，但如何从这些量子态中高效恢复经典信息（读出限制）仍然研究不足。这一读出瓶颈阻碍了量子PDE算法在反演问题中的端到端应用。

Method: 提出量子-经典混合框架：量子处理器演化PDE并带采样噪声地评估损失函数，经典计算机通过高斯过程回归调优超参数以探索下一个试验候选。针对线性和半线性自治演化PDE，采用归一化量子态损失作为数据失配函数，结合量子PDE求解器与Hadamard测试评估新失配，无需重构完整解向量。

Result: 误差传播和复杂度分析表明新数据失配函数在量子测量下优于传统L2损失。对1D和2D线性对流扩散方程及非线性受迫粘性Burgers方程的数值实验验证了该框架在采样噪声影响下的参数反演可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于PDE的反演问题提供了可行的量子辅助方案，展示了量子PDE算法在完整量-端到端优化栈中的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum computing offers potential advantages for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, most existing quantum PDE solvers primarily focus on preparing quantum states for solutions, while the efficient recovery of classical information from these states remains less explored. Motivated by the readout limitation, we propose a quantum-classical hybrid framework for Bayesian PDE inversion problems: The quantum processor evolves the PDE and evaluate the loss function with sampling noises, while the classical computer tunes the hyper-parameters in the Gaussian Process Regression to explore the next trial candidate. To match the quantum solvers for linear and semi-linear autonomous evolution PDEs, we suggest to use a normalized quantum-state loss as the data-misfit function and evaluate the new misfit by combining quantum PDE solvers with the Hadamard test, thereby allowing us to extract useful classical information using only a limited number of quantum state copies without reconstructing the full solution vector. The analysis of error propagation and overall complexity of loss evaluation under a prescribed accuracy shows that the new data-misfit function outperforms the conventional L2-loss under quantum measurements. Quantum circuit simulations of 1D and 2D linear convection diffusion equations under approximate and finite sampling loss evaluations, together with classical numerical experiments on a nonlinear forced viscous Burgers equation, demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach for parameter inversion even when the loss evaluations are affected by sampling noise. This framework may provide a viable quantum-assisted scheme for PDE-based inverse problems and elucidate the potential of quantum PDE algorithms in addressing a complete quantum-to-end optimization stack.

</details>


### [126] [Primal-dual methods and acceleration for Morozov and equality constrained regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27106)
*Diana-Elena Mirciu,Martin Benning,Elena Resmerita*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对带噪声数据的线性逆问题，在Hilbert空间和Banach空间中系统分析了非加速与加速原始-对偶方法的正则化性质，分别建立了基于Bregman距离和范数的误差估计，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性逆问题在噪声数据下的重构性分析是反问题领域的关键问题，此前对Condat-Vũ算法和加速原始-对偶混合梯度法在一般凸数据保真度及Banach空间下的正则化误差定量分析尚不充分，缺乏统一的误差估计框架。

Method: 对非加速Condat-Vũ算法，在Hilbert空间中基于Bregman距离推导误差估计，并扩展到Banach空间（含非自反空间，用于非负解重构）；对加速原始-对偶混合梯度法，建立范数意义下的误差估计。方法适用于满足适当扰动条件的一般凸数据保真度，这些条件在等式约束和Morozov正则化下得到显式验证。

Result: 推导出两类算法的正则化误差界，恢复经典情形下的已知行为，同时将正则化分析推广到更一般的框架（包括非欧几里得几何和非自反Banach空间设置）。数值实验采用稀疏正则化器和熵模型，验证了理论结果的有效性及噪声下实际性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为带噪声线性逆问题的原始-对偶方法提供了统一且更一般的正则化分析框架，同时覆盖Hilbert与Banach空间设置，弥补了加速方法在一般凸数据保真和Banach空间下误差分析的空缺。

Abstract: This work develops a regularization analysis of non-accelerated and accelerated primal-dual methods for solving linear inverse problems in the presence of noisy data. We investigate a Condat-Vũ algorithm and an accelerated primal-dual hybrid gradient method in Hilbert spaces, with focus on quantifying the effect of data perturbations on the reconstruction error. For the non-accelerated scheme, we derive error estimates in terms of Bregman distances, whereas for the accelerated scheme we establish error estimates in norm. The study accommodates a general class of convex data fidelities satisfying suitable perturbation conditions, which are verified explicitly for equality constrained and Morozov regularization. For the non-accelerated method, the analysis is further extended to Banach spaces, taking into account non-Euclidean geometries and including a particular non-reflexive setting tailored to nonnegative solution reconstruction. The results recover known behavior in classical settings while extending the regularization analysis to these more general frameworks. Numerical experiments with representative regularizers, including sparsity and entropic models, support the theoretical findings and illustrate practical performance under noise.

</details>


### [127] [A multi-class kinetic traffic flow model: discrete-velocity formulation and diffusively-corrected macroscopic limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27159)
*Carmen Mezquita-Nieto,Paola Goatin,Axel Klar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非局部Prigogine-Herman框架的多类别离散速度动力学交通流模型，推导了双曲缩放系统，证明了任意车辆类别数下离散化模型的双曲性和全线性退化性，并采用路径守恒有限体积方法进行数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学交通流模型多为单一种类，难以刻画不同类型车辆（如轿车、卡车）间的相互作用差异。本文旨在将非局部Prigogine-Herman框架扩展至多类别场景，以更真实地模拟混合交通流中不同车辆类别的制动与松弛行为。

Method: 从连续动力学公式出发，通过双曲缩放导出多类别系统方程组；对速度变量进行离散化以支持任意数量的车辆类别；分析离散化系统的结构性质（证明双曲性和全线性退化性）；由于模型具有非守恒结构，采用路径守恒有限体积格式进行数值逼近；进一步推导扩散修正的宏观多类别模型并分析其稳定性。

Result: 成功推导并离散化了多类别动力学交通流模型，证明了系统的双曲性和全线性退化性；建立了路径守恒有限体积数值格式；推导了扩散修正的宏观模型并验证了其稳定性；通过单车道数值模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为多类别混合交通流的动力学建模提供了完整的理论框架与可靠的数值工具，其扩散修正宏观模型在稳定性分析中表现良好，为实际交通流模拟与应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces a multi-class extension of a discrete-velocity kinetic traffic flow model based on a non-local Prigogine-Herman framework. We derive a hyperbolically scaled system of equations from a continuous kinetic formulation describing interactions between different vehicle classes through braking and relaxation terms. The model is then discretized with respect to the velocity variable for an arbitrary number of vehicle classes, and the structural properties of the resulting formulation are analyzed. In particular, we prove hyperbolicity and total linear degeneracy. Due to the non-conservative structure of the model, we employ a path-conservative finite volume scheme for the numerical approximation of the system. Finally, we derive the corresponding diffusively-corrected macroscopic multi-class model, investigate its stability and present numerical simulations on a single-lane road to illustrate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [128] [Bochner Stability for B-stable DIRK Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27210)
*Anthony E. Ramirez,Abner J. Salgado*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了二阶段和三阶段对角隐式Runge-Kutta（DIRK）格式中U-稳定性与B-（代数）稳定性之间的等价关系，并基于此证明了B-稳定方法的Bochner范数先验估计。作为应用，在最小正则性条件下通过能量估计和紧性证明了U-稳定DIRK格式对线性强制演化问题的收敛性，并将其应用于梯度流，推导出离散局部能量耗散不等式，同时构造反例展示了阶段能量单调性的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Salgado和Tomas在三阶对角隐式Runge-Kutta（DIRK）格式中引入了U-稳定性的概念，但U-稳定性与经典的B-（代数）稳定性之间的关系尚未明确。建立二者的等价关系可以为B-稳定方法提供合适的Bochner范数先验估计，从而在最小正则性条件下证明演化问题的收敛性，并进一步推广到梯度流等非线性问题。

Method: 本文通过理论分析建立二阶段和三阶段DIRK格式中U-稳定性与B-（代数）稳定性的等价性。基于该等价性，利用能量估计和紧性论证方法，在线性强制演化问题的最小正则性假设下证明收敛性；随后将所建立的离散框架应用于梯度流，推导离散局部能量耗散不等式，并通过构造反例验证阶段能量单调性的局限。

Result: 成功证明了二阶段和三阶段DIRK格式中U-稳定性与B-（代数）稳定性的等价性，从而获得了B-稳定方法的Bochner范数先验估计。在最小正则性条件下证明了U-稳定DIRK格式对线性强制演化问题的收敛性，建立了梯度流问题的离散局部能量耗散不等式，并给出了说明阶段能量单调性局限性的反例。

Conclusion: 本文搭建了U-稳定性与经典代数稳定性理论之间的桥梁，为B-稳定DIRK格式提供了Bochner范数框架下的理论保证，拓展了其在最小正则性演化问题及梯度流中的应用范围。同时，通过反例揭示了阶段能量单调性并非普遍成立的结论，为后续研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: In Abner J. Salgado and Ignacio Tomas. Diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta schemes: discrete energy-balance laws and compactness properties. J. Number. Math., 31(4):313-341, 2023, the notion of $U$-stability for Diagonally Implicit Runge-Kutta (DIRK) schemes was introduced. Here we establish the equivalence between $U$- and $B$- (algebraic) stability for two- and three-stage DIRK schemes, which then} provides suitable Bochner norm a priori estimates for $B$-stable methods. As applications, we first prove the convergence of $U$-stable DIRK schemes for linear coercive evolution problems under minimal regularity via energy estimates and compactness. Second, we apply these discretizations to gradient flows, which allow us to derive discrete local energy dissipation inequalities and provide counterexamples that demonstrate the limitations of stagewise energy monotonicity.

</details>


### [129] [Enforcing Dirichlet Boundary Conditions in Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27256)
*Andrew M. Stuart,Margaret Trautner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的神经算子架构，通过在每一层将输出限制在输出域上拉普拉斯算子齐次Dirichlet特征函数的张成子空间中，使得网络在训练前就严格满足齐次Dirichlet边界条件，同时保持核积分神经算子的表达能力。该方法仅要求输出域有界且边界Lipschitz连续，不限制离散化方式，适用于任意网格和一般几何。作者证明了该架构的普适逼近性质，并统一了多种算子学习方法的现有理论。在二维Darcy流和Helmholtz方程的系数到解映射上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子架构大多通过训练数据隐式地学习边界条件，而非精确地强制执行边界条件；已有的显式边界条件方法存在不实用的限制，如要求边界光滑、均匀网格、可分盒状域等。作者希望提出一种既能精确满足齐次Dirichlet边界条件、又保持神经算子表达能力的架构，同时避免上述限制。

Method: 所提方法通过强制每一层输出落在输出域上拉普拉斯算子齐次Dirichlet特征函数子集的张成空间中，从架构设计上保证输出满足齐次Dirichlet边界条件，与训练无关。该方法仅要求输出域为具有Lipschitz边界的有界域，对离散化方式无限制，适用于任意网格与一般几何形状。作者证明该架构具有普适逼近性质，并通过该分析统一证明了广泛核积分神经算子类的普适性。

Result: 在二维PDE的系数到解映射上进行了验证：方形域上的Darcy流和圆形域上的Helmholtz方程。与替代方法进行了比较，验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法在满足齐次Dirichlet边界条件的同时保持核积分神经算子的表达能力和普适逼近性质，且适用于任意离散化和一般几何域，为显式强制执行边界条件的神经算子研究提供了新方向，并统一了多种算子学习方法的理论框架。

Abstract: Operator learning in scientific machine learning is concerned with approximation of maps between infinite-dimensional function spaces; such maps frequently arise as the solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). Neural operators have demonstrated broad empirical success at approximating such maps from data. However, most existing neural operator architectures enforce boundary conditions indirectly through training from data even though the boundary condition is often known exactly. Furthermore, existing modifications and approaches that do enforce boundary conditions explicitly suffer from impractical restrictions, including boundary smoothness, uniform grids, and separable, box-like domains. In this work, we propose an architecture which, independently of training, satisfies homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, whilst simultaneously retaining the expressivity of existing kernel-integral neural operator architectures. This is achieved by enforcing the property that the output of each layer is contained in the span of a subset of the homogeneous Dirichlet eigenfunctions of the Laplacian on the output domain. The method requires only that the output domain be bounded with Lipschitz boundary and places no restriction on the choice of discretization, making it applicable to arbitrary mesh data and general geometries. We prove universal approximation for the resulting architecture; furthermore the approach we adopt in the analysis proves universality for a broad class of kernel-integral neural operators thereby uniting existing theory for a variety of operator learning methods. We validate the proposed method on maps defined by the coefficient to solution map in 2D PDEs: Darcy flow on a square domain and the Helmholtz equation on a circular domain. Comparisons are made with alternative methods.

</details>


### [130] [A Multilevel Interacting Particle System Method for the estimation of Failure Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27275)
*Rubén Aylwin,José Pinto*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将多层级分解与交互粒子系统框架相结合的新方法，用于计算给定感兴趣量超过或低于预设阈值的概率。该算法通过序贯抽样方案，实现了比经典多层蒙特卡洛方法更快的收敛速度，并与当前最先进技术相媲美，同时具有维度无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多层蒙特卡洛方法在计算稀有事件概率或阈值超越概率时收敛速度有限，需要更高效且维度无关的算法来加速此类计算。

Method: 将多层级分解（multilevel decomposition）与交互粒子系统（Interacting Particle Systems）框架相结合，通过应用序贯抽样方案（sequential sampling scheme）来估计感兴趣量跨越预设定阈值的概率。

Result: 算法实现了比经典多层蒙特卡洛方法更快的收敛速度，与当前最先进技术相当，且具有维度无关性；生成的样本点序列集中于感兴趣量的临界值附近。理论结果经过严格证明并通过数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 该方法在效率和维度处理方面均优于经典多层蒙特卡洛算法，是一种有竞争力的概率估计新方案，其收敛性质有严格的数学保证。

Abstract: We propose a novel method that combines a multilevel decomposition with the framework of Interacting Particle Systems to compute the probability that given quantities of interest take values above or below a certain pre-specified value. Through the application of a sequential sampling scheme, the algorithm is able to achieve convergence rates faster than those achieved by the classical Multilevel Monte Carlo algorithm, and competes with the current state of the art techniques, while also being dimension independent. Furthermore, the sample points generated by the algorithm form a sequence which concentrates near the critical value of the quantity of interest. Our results are rigorously established and later verified through numerical experiments.

</details>

### [131] [Reservoir: A Large-Scale Simulated Dataset for Training and Evaluating Epidemiological Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27408)
*Carson Dudley,Reiden Magdaleno,Marisa Eisenberg*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Reservoir，一个大规模开放模拟器及数据集，包含50万条疫情爆发轨迹（总计10亿模拟天），覆盖多样病原特征、人群结构和干预方案。每条轨迹都带有完整的真实标签，包括真实感染数、时变再生数和反事实干预效果等现实中无法直接观测的量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病流行病学正越来越多地采用AI方法进行预测、监测和暴发分析，但可用的训练时序数据规模比其他领域（如蛋白质结构预测、NLP）小了数个数量级。由于真实流行病学数据无法实际达到训练大规模AI方法所需的规模，模拟数据成为可行的替代方案。

Method: Reservoir由一个带真实噪声和报告伪影的随机模拟器生成，并包含可配置时间、依从性和年龄依赖效力的干预措施。当前版本包含50万条爆发轨迹，覆盖10亿个模拟天，横跨多样的病原特征、人群结构和干预制度。

Result: 该数据集提供了真实世界数据集无法提供的反事实实验、监测设计研究和流行病模型的规模化训练能力。每条轨迹都带有完整的真实标签，包括真实感染数、时变有效再生数和反事实干预效应等。

Conclusion: Reservoir通过大规模模拟填补了流行病学AI方法训练数据不足的空白，使疫情建模能够在真实数据无法企及的规模上进行训练和研究。

Abstract: Large-scale, standardized datasets have driven many advances in AI-based scientific modeling, from protein structure prediction to natural language processing. Infectious disease epidemiology is increasingly adopting AI methods for forecasting, surveillance, and outbreak analytics, but the time-series data available to train them remains orders of magnitude smaller than the corpora behind the advances seen in other fields. Because the scope of real-world epidemiological data cannot practically reach the scale needed to train truly large-scale AI methods, simulated data provides a possible alternative. Here we introduce Reservoir, a large open simulator and dataset of realistic epidemic simulations in which every trajectory carries complete ground-truth labels, including quantities that cannot be measured directly in a real outbreak, such as true infection counts, time-varying reproduction numbers, and counterfactual intervention effects. Reservoir is generated by a stochastic simulator with realistic noise and reporting artifacts, together with interventions with configurable timing, compliance, and age-dependent efficacy. The current release contains 500,000 outbreak trajectories spanning one billion simulated days across diverse pathogen characteristics, population structures, and intervention regimes. Reservoir enables counterfactual experiments, surveillance-design studies, and training of epidemic models at a scale real-world datasets cannot provide.

</details>

### [132] [Closed geodesics in homology classes modulo sublattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26311)
*Noam Pirani*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Weil-Petersson随机双曲曲面在亏格趋于无穷时的原始闭测地线在模格子Γ的同调类中的分布，通过对素指标格子的平均，计算了加权计数函数的所有中心矩，并展示了泊松与高斯区域之间的相变，以及未归一化方差的渐近行为，与算术级数中素数的分布类似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机双曲曲面上原始闭测地线在给定同调模格子下的分布规律，特别关注大亏格极限下的统计行为，并与数论中素数在算术级数中的分布现象建立类比，揭示几何与数论之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用Weil-Petersson度量下的随机双曲曲面模型，对亏格为g的曲面取素指标q的格子Γ⊂Z^{2g}的平均，研究原始闭测地线在同调类mod Γ中的加权计数函数；通过计算所有中心矩来分析泊松-高斯相变，并研究未归一化方差G_M(X,Γ)在X→∞且大亏格极限下的渐近行为。

Result: 当q→∞时，对所有指标为q的格子取平均，加权计数函数的所有中心矩均可计算；当X/(q log X)→λ>0时呈现泊松统计，当X/(q log X)→∞时呈现高斯统计，显示了从泊松到高斯的相变；未归一化方差G_M(X,Γ)在X→∞且大亏格极限下渐近于X log X。

Conclusion: 随机双曲曲面上原始闭测地线在同调类模格子Γ下的分布展现出泊松-高斯相变现象，其方差渐近行为与素数在算术级数中的分布高度类比，表明模曲面几何中同调类测地线计数与数论中素数分布之间存在深刻的平行结构。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a Weil-Petersson random hyperbolic surface of genus $g$, and let $Γ\subset \mathbb{Z}^{2g}$ be a lattice of prime index $q$. We study the distribution of primitive closed geodesics in homology classes mod $Γ$ in the large genus limit. Averaging over all lattices of index $q$, with $q \to \infty$, we compute all the centered moments of the corresponding weighted counting functions, and exhibit a transition between Poisson and Gaussian regimes (depending on whether $\frac{X}{q\log X}$, the expected number of primitive geodesics in a given homology class mod $Γ$, tends to $λ>0$ or $\infty$). We also study the unnormalized variance $G_M(X,Γ)$ of the counts among homology classes, and show that as $X \to \infty$, averaged over all lattices of prime index $q$, it is asymptotic to $X\log X$ in the large genus limit. These results are analogous to phenomena arising in the distribution of primes in arithmetic progressions.

</details>


### [133] [Discrete dynamics of Eratosthenes sieve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26384)
*Fred B. Holt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将厄拉托斯特尼筛法建模为离散动力系统，利用连续素数阶乘循环间的递归关系，将素数间隙和星座（constellation）的种群数量建模为马尔可夫链，获得其精确模型。通过除去超指数增长得到相对种群的精确模型，其渐近值为常数且仅依赖于跨越区间的奇素因子。在均匀分布假设下，给出区间(p_k^2, p_{k+1}^2]内星座出现数量的一阶估计（二次密度统计量），素数间隙的估计值与至5.677×10^14的样本数据吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析数论方法处理素数分布及其结构（如素数间隙、素数星座）往往难以获得精确模型。本文希望将筛法视为一个离散动力系统，从数学机制本身出发推导出素数间隙和星座种群数量的精确递归模型与渐近行为，从而为素数分布规律提供新的动力学视角和精确预测工具。

Method: 将厄拉托斯特尼筛法建模为离散动态系统：每个阶段对应一个长度φ(p^#)、跨度p^#的间隙循环G(p^#)，并通过递归G(p_k^#)→G(p_{k+1}^#)构造下一阶段。以G(p_0^#)为初始条件，对跨度|s|<2p_1的所有星座，证明不同长度的驱动项构成马尔可夫链，从而得到n_s(p_k^#)的精确模型。对可容许星座s，将其种群n_{s,J}(p^#)除以∏(q-J-1)去除超指数增长，定义相对种群w_s(p_k^#)及其精确递推模型。利用均匀分布假设，定义二次密度统计量η_s(p_k)，给出(p_k^2, p_{k+1}^2]区间内星座出现数量的一阶估计。

Result: 相对种群的渐近值w_{s,J}(∞)是常数且≥1，仅依赖于s的跨度的奇素因子。对素数间隙g，基于二次密度η_g(p)的一阶估计η̂_g(p)与直至5.677×10^14的实测样本数据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作表明筛法本身蕴含着马尔可夫链结构，为素数间隙和星座分布提供了精确的动力学模型和有效的一阶估计方法。这一框架不仅解释了大范围内素数分布的结构特征，还为素数星座计数提供了可计算的新途径。

Abstract: We study Eratosthenes sieve as a discrete dynamic system. At each stage of the sieve there is a cycle of gaps ${\mathcal G}(p^\#)$ of length $φ(p^\#)$ and span $p^\#$. There is a recursion ${\mathcal G}(p_k^\#)\longrightarrow {\mathcal G}(p_{k+1}^\#)$ that creates the next cycle from the current one.
  If we take initial conditions from the cycle ${\mathcal G}(p_0^\#)$, then for all constellations of span $|s| < 2p_1$, including gaps $g < 2p_1$, the driving terms of various lengths form Markov chains. These yield {\it exact} models for the populations $n_s(p_k^\#)$ for all further stages of the sieve.
  If $s$ is an admissible constellation of length $J$, then its population $n_{s,J}(p^\#)$ grows as $Θ\left( \prod (q-J-1)\right)$. So we factor out the superexponential growth to obtain the exact model for the relative population $w_s(p_k^\#)$ of the constellation $s$ across all further stages of the sieve. $$ w_{s,J}(p_k^\#) \; = \; n_{s,J}(p_k^\#) \, / \, \prod_{J+1 < p \le p_k} (p-J-1) $$ The asymptotic value of the relative population is a constant ${w_{s,J}(\infty) \ge 1}$ that depends only on the odd prime factors that divide a span in $s$.
  Assuming that the instances of a constellation $s$ are approximately uniformly distributed in ${\mathcal G}(p_k^\#)$, we develop first-order estimates of the number of instances $s$ that would occur in the interval of survival $ΔH(p_k) = (p_k^2, p_{k+1}^2]$. We define a statistic $η_s(p_k)$, the quadratic density of the constellation $s$ over the interval $ΔH(p_k)$. We show that the first-order estimates $\widehat{η_g}(p)$ for prime gaps agree with samples up to $5.677\,E14$.

</details>


### [134] [$k$-Lucas Annulus for Polynomial Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26452)
*Herbert Batte*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个二项式恒等式，将k-Lucas数与k-Fibonacci序列联系起来：当n为偶数时，k-Lucas数的二项式加权和可约化为L_{k,mn}的倍数；当n为奇数时，则约化为F_{k,mn}的倍数。基于此恒等式，结合Dalal和Govil的环域原理，得到了包含n次复多项式所有零点的k-Lucas环域估计，补充了现有的k-Fibonacci零点界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多项式零点界（如Díaz-Barrero、Bidkham-Shashahani和Kaur的结果）主要基于k-Fibonacci数，缺乏基于k-Lucas数的对称性估计。作者希望建立k-Lucas数与k-Fibonacci序列之间的精确二项式关系，从而推导出新的k-Lucas环域零点界。

Method: 首先证明核心二项式恒等式：对k>0、m≥1、n≥1，k-Lucas数的二项式加权和在n为偶数时等于L_{k,mn}的倍数，n为奇数时等于F_{k,mn}的倍数。这一奇偶性差异源于k-Lucas数的Binet公式缺少k-Fibonacci数Binet公式中1/(α-β)的正规化因子。然后利用该恒等式为每个n的奇偶性和每个m构造一组和为1的正权重，结合Dalal和Govil的广义环域原理，推导出多项式零点的k-Lucas环域。

Result: 得到了一个全新的k-Lucas环域包含所有复多项式零点的结果，该结果与基于k-Fibonacci数的零点界互补，并展示了由n的奇偶性决定的两种不同形式（偶数时涉及L_{k,mn}，奇数时涉及F_{k,mn}）。

Conclusion: 本文建立的二项式恒等式揭示了k-Lucas与k-Fibonacci序列之间的深层代数联系，并成功将其应用于多项式零点分布问题，提供了新的几何估计工具。这一结果补充并拓展了现有文献中基于k-Fibonacci数的零点界。

Abstract: We prove a binomial identity relating the $k$-Lucas and $k$-Fibonacci sequences: for real $k>0$ and integers $m\ge1$, $n\ge1$, a binomial-weighted sum of $k$-Lucas numbers reduces to a multiple of $L_{k,mn}$ when $n$ is even, but to a multiple of $F_{k,mn}$ when $n$ is odd. This parity dependence traces back to the $k$-Lucas Binet formula lacking the normalising factor $1/(α-β)$ present for $k$-Fibonacci numbers. The identity supplies, for each parity of $n$ and each $m$, an explicit family of positive weights summing to one; combined with the general annulus principle of Dalal and Govil, this yields a $k$-Lucas annulus containing all the zeros of a complex polynomial of degree $n$, complementing the $k$-Fibonacci-based bounds of Díaz-Barrero, Bidkham-Shashahani, and Kaur.

</details>


### [135] [A Neighbouring-Denominator Case of the Erdős--Mahler Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26455)
*Diego Marques*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Erdős和Mahler在1939年提出的一个与连分数收敛子相关的猜想的一个邻域分母情形：若对无穷多个n，有P(p_n q_n q_{n+1})有界，则ξ必为Liouville数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős和Mahler在1939年提出了一个关于无理数收敛子的大素数因子与Liouville数之间关系的猜想，该猜想至今未完全解决。本文旨在证明该猜想的一个特殊情况。

Method: 证明结合了两个关键技术：一是相邻收敛子之间的行列式恒等式（determinant identity），二是p-adic对数线性形式的固定基数估计。

Result: 成功证明了若P(p_n q_n q_{n+1})对无穷多个n有界，则ξ是Liouville数，即证明了原猜想的一个邻域分母情形的成立。

Conclusion: 该结果推进了Erdős-Mahler猜想的研究，将一个相关但更强的条件（包含相邻分母）下的情形给出了肯定解答。

Abstract: In 1939, Erdős and Mahler conjectured that an irrational real number $ξ$ must be a Liouville number whenever $P(p_nq_n)$ is bounded for infinitely many convergents $p_n/q_n$, where $P(N)$ denotes the largest prime factor of a nonzero integer $N$. In this note, we prove a neighbouring-denominator case of their conjecture: if $P(p_nq_nq_{n+1})$ is bounded for infinitely many $n$, then $ξ$ is a Liouville number. The proof combines the determinant identity for consecutive convergents with a fixed-base estimate for linear forms in $p$-adic logarithms.

</details>


### [136] [Period Relations for Theta Products and Elliptic Integral Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26514)
*Dianbin Bao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于周期多项式的框架，用于研究模形式临界L值之间的关系，这些关系源于theta积与椭圆积分矩，并为已知的完全椭圆积分矩之间的有理关系提供了概念性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于完全椭圆积分矩之间的有理关系是通过解析或实验方法发现的，缺乏统一的概念性框架来解释这些关系背后的数学结构。

Method: 提出周期多项式（period-polynomial）方法，将模形式临界L值之间的关系与theta积和椭圆积分矩联系起来，通过周期关系系统推导这些有理关系。

Result: 周期关系为多个已知的完全椭圆积分矩间的有理关系提供了概念性解释，统一了此前通过解析或实验方法获得的结果。

Conclusion: 周期多项式方法为理解模形式临界L值与椭圆积分矩之间的内在联系提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a period-polynomial approach to relations among critical L-values of modular forms arising from theta products and elliptic integral moments. In particular, period relations provide a conceptual explanation for several known rational relations among moments of complete elliptic integrals that had previously been discovered through analytic or experimental methods.

</details>


### [137] [The k-Rank Taylor Family: Canonical Harmonic Lifts, Principal Parts, and Rademacher Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26617)
*Seokho Jin,Sihun Jo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Garvan修正k秩矩与奇数阶Appell函数的椭圆Taylor系数之间的编码关系。证明所有非全纯部分可由单个权3/2向量值调和Maass形式的规范高Serre导数的标量收缩产生，并通过前d=k-1个Appell修正选择唯一提升，得到所有偶阶修正矩的精确递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于固定k≥2，Garvan修正k秩矩被奇数阶Appell函数的椭圆Taylor系数编码，但完成的2n+1阶系数具有权2n+3/2，无法实现为固定权的向量值模形式。作者试图突破这一障碍，寻找统一的框架来理解整个修正矩族。

Method: 利用奇数阶Appell函数与谐和Maass形式之间的关系，证明非全纯部分可由单个权3/2向量值调和Maass形式的规范高Serre导数的标量收缩产生。前d=k-1个Appell修正被用作弱全纯歧义上的坐标，选择唯一提升。通过Maass-Poincaré提升的Rademacher展开与典范提升的cusp形式修正，推导精确递推关系。

Result: 初始Taylor数据无需利用修正矩级数的正Fourier系数即可恢复主部，主部决定阴影。Maass-Poincaré提升的正系数具有收敛的Rademacher展开，典范提升与之相差唯一的cusp形式。对k=2，cusp形式修正消失，得到涉及收敛Kloosterman-Bessel级数的2-标记Durfee符号数的精确公式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了修正k秩矩与谐和Maass形式理论之间的深层联系，给出了所有偶阶修正矩的精确递推，并为k=2的情形提供了Durfee符号数的闭式精确公式。

Abstract: For fixed $k\ge2$, Garvan's modified $k$-rank moments are encoded by odd elliptic Taylor coefficients of an odd-level Appell function. The completed coefficient of order $2n+1$ has weight $2n+3/2$, so the family cannot be realized as a fixed-weight vector-valued modular form. Nevertheless, all non-holomorphic parts arise from scalar contractions of canonical higher Serre derivatives of a single weight-$3/2$ vector-valued harmonic Maass form. Among lifts with the prescribed unary-theta shadow, the first $d=k-1$ Appell corrections are coordinates on the weakly holomorphic ambiguity and select a unique lift; the next correction is nonzero.
  The initial Taylor data recover the principal part without using positive Fourier coefficients of the modified moment series, and the principal part determines the shadow. The positive coefficients of the corresponding Maass--Poincaré lift have convergent Rademacher expansions; the canonical lift differs from it by a unique cusp form. This yields exact recursions for all even modified moments. For $k=2$, the cusp-form adjustment vanishes, giving an exact formula for the numbers of $2$-marked Durfee symbols involving a convergent Kloosterman--Bessel series.

</details>


### [138] [$\mathbb{F}_q$-linear relations among Thakur's multiple zeta values in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26636)
*Jinyuan Hu,Hanqing Huang,Li Lai,Kunyue Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究Thakur多重zeta值在有限域F_q上的线性结构。作者证明了权重w的F_q-线性子空间Z_w的维数生成函数具有明确的封闭形式表达式，并构造了Z_w的显式F_q-基。同时证明了Carlitz多重polylogarithm值之间的任意F_q-线性关系都可以表示为quadruple-carry关系的F_q-线性组合，这是对Chang-Chen-Mishiba和Im-Kim-Le-Ngo Dac-Pham关于F_q(θ)情形定理的F_q-类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在理解Thakur多重zeta值在F_q-线性意义下的代数结构，具体而言是确定各权重下这些值的F_q-维数并构造显式基。这项工作将已知的F_q(θ)-定理推广到F_q-线性情形，提供了一个完整的维度公式和基构造，并引入quadruple-carry关系作为经典多重zeta值double-shuffle关系的F_q-类比。

Method: 作者利用F_q-linear子空间Z_w^(F_q)的结构，通过引入quadruple-carry关系来刻画Carlitz多重polylogarithm值之间的线性关系。证明的核心工具包括维数生成函数的推导以及显式基的构造。文中方法受Im-Kim-Ngo Dac近期工作的启发，将double-shuffle关系的思想转化为F_q-线性设置下的quadruple-carry关系。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明了维数生成函数的封闭公式：∑_{w≥1} (dim_{F_q} Z_w^(F_q)) x^w = x(1-x^q)(1-2x+x^q)/(1-2x+x^{q+1})^2；(2) 构造了Z_w^(F_q)的显式F_q-基；(3) 证明了任意F_q-线性关系均为quadruple-carry关系的线性组合。

Conclusion: 该结果完成了对Thakur多重zeta值在F_q-线性层面结构的完整刻画，可作为Chang-Chen-Mishiba以及Im-Kim-Le-Ngo Dac-Pham所得F_q(θ)-定理的F_q-类比。quadruple-carry关系作为double-shuffle关系的F_q-版本，揭示了有限域特征下多重zeta值的内在线性结构。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{Z}_w^{(\mathbb{F}_q)}$ be the $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear subspace of $\mathbb{F}_q(\!(θ^{-1})\!)$ spanned by Thakur's multiple zeta values $ζ_A(\mathfrak{s})$ of weight $w$. We prove that $\sum_{w=1}^{\infty} \left(\dim_{\mathbb{F}_q} \mathcal{Z}_w^{(\mathbb{F}_q)}\right) x^w = \frac{x(1-x^q)(1-2x+x^q)}{(1-2x+x^{q+1})^2}$. Moreover, we construct an explicit $\mathbb{F}_q$-basis of $\mathcal{Z}_w^{(\mathbb{F}_q)}$, and prove that any $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear relation among Carlitz multiple polylogarithm values $\operatorname{Li}_A(\mathfrak{s})$ is an $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear combination of quadruple-carry relations. This result can be regarded as an $\mathbb{F}_q$-analogue of the corresponding $\mathbb{F}_q(θ)$-theorem proved by Chang--Chen--Mishiba and independently by Im--Kim--Le--Ngo Dac--Pham.
  Our discovery of the quadruple-carry relations is inspired by the recent work of Im--Kim--Ngo Dac. These relations may be viewed as $\mathbb{F}_q$-analogues of the double-shuffle relations among classical multiple zeta values $ζ(\mathfrak{s})$.

</details>


### [139] [Torsion-vanishing for Siegel modular varieties via supercuspidal congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26721)
*Ana Caraiani,Sug Woo Shin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了带挠系数的Siegel模簇上同调的generic部分集中在中度以上的次数（在无分歧情形下该generic性是最优的）。方法依赖于Igusa叠的相对上同调的半perpverse性，以及由Scholze和Fintzen--Shin引入的同余技巧所实现的迹公式计算。相比于Yang--Zhu通过范畴局部Langlands处理abelian型Shimura簇的工作，本文的方法针对Siegel模簇定制，但能处理任意小特征的系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Siegel模簇带挠系数的上同调结构是Langlands纲领中的重要问题。已有工作（如Yang--Zhu）基于范畴局部Langlands处理abelian型Shimura簇，但受限于系数特征不能过小。本文旨在提供一种适用于Siegel模簇且能处理任意小特征系数的方法，证明其generic部分上同调集中在中度以上。

Method: 核心方法包括：（1）利用Igusa叠的相对上同调的半perpverse性质来获得上同调集中度的下界信息；（2）采用Scholze与Fintzen--Shin发展的同余技巧，将问题归结为可计算的迹公式；（3）在无分歧情形下证明所采用的generic性概念是最优的。

Result: 证明了在合适的generic性假设下（无分歧情形下最优），Siegel模簇带挠系数的上同调的generic部分集中在中度以上的次数。该方法相比Yang--Zhu的工作，能够处理任意小特征的系数。

Conclusion: 本文为Siegel模簇带挠系数的上同调结构提供了新的证明途径，尤其扩展了可处理系数特征的范围。这一结果对理解Siegel模簇的几何与算术性质，以及更广泛的Langlands纲领具有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove that the generic part of the cohomology of Siegel modular varieties with torsion coefficients is concentrated above the middle degree, under a suitable notion of genericity that is optimal in the unramified case. Our method relies on the semi-perversity of the relative cohomology of the Igusa stack and on a trace formula computation that is made possible by a congruence technique introduced by Scholze and Fintzen--Shin. Compared with the work of Yang--Zhu, which handles Shimura varieties of abelian type via categorical local Langlands, our method is adapted to Siegel modular varieties but handles coefficients of arbitrarily small characteristic.

</details>


### [140] [Specialisations of the Burungale-Castella-Skinner main conjecture to $\mathbb Z_p$-lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26791)
*Ki-Seng Tan,Fabien Trihan,Kwok-Wing Tsoi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文结合Burungale-Castella-Skinner的二元主猜想与第一作者的特化公式，在L/K的每条Zp-线上建立了特征理想等式（含显式局部因子），定义了"例外线"（二元Perrin-Riou元素特化为零的情形），证明其只有有限多条，并给出数量上界（不超过秩，秩一时唯一例外线为反cyclotomic线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二元Iwasawa主猜想推广到所有Zp-线性特化上，处理Perrin-Riou元素特化为零的退化情形，并精确刻画例外线的分布，深化对椭圆曲线在虚二次域上Zp^2-扩张中算术性质的理解。

Method: ①结合Burungale-Castella-Skinner的积分二元主猜想与第一作者的特殊化公式；②定义非挠模的特征理想为零以涵盖例外情形；③对例外的Zp-线进行有限性证明并用cyclotomic增广阶给出数量上界；④结合Tate-Shafarevich群的有限性与cyclotomic p-adic高度配对非退化性给出更精细的秩相关上界。

Result: 在每个Zp-线上建立含显式局部因子的特征理想恒等式；证明例外线仅有有限多条；cyclotomic线非例外且局部因子平凡，anticyclotomic线例外；例外线数量≤cyclotomic增广阶，且在Tate-Shafarevich群有限及高度配对非退化的条件下，例外线数量≤rank(E(K))，秩一时anticyclotomic线是唯一例外线。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了二元主猜想在Zp-线特化下的行为，证明例外线稀少（有限多条）且可被秩界约束，为Zp^2-扩张中的Iwasawa理论提供了新的结构定理。

Abstract: Let $p>3$ be a prime, $E/\mathbb Q$ be an elliptic curve and $K$ an imaginary quadratic field satisfying the hypotheses of Burungale-Castella-Skinner, and let $L/K$ be the unique $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$-extension. In this note, by combining their integral two-variable main conjecture with the specialisation formula of the first-named author, we obtain a characteristic-ideal identity over every $\mathbb{Z}_p$-line in $L/K$, involving an explicit local factor. With the characteristic ideal of a non-torsion module defined to be zero, this identity also applies when the two-variable Perrin-Riou element specialises to zero. We call such lines exceptional and prove that only finitely many occur. We also prove that the cyclotomic line is non-exceptional with trivial local factor, and that the anticyclotomic line is exceptional. Finally, we bound the number of exceptional lines by the cyclotomic augmentation order and prove that if the $p$-primary Tate-Shafarevich group over $K$ is finite and the cyclotomic $p$-adic height pairing is non-degenerate, then this number is at most ${\rm rank}(E(K))$. In particular, when $E(K)$ has rank one, the anticyclotomic line is the unique exceptional line.

</details>


### [141] [Two-point correlations of multiplicative functions with dense orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26814)
*Néo Tardy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于两个具有稠密像的完全乘性函数f,g，除非它们是某种特殊形式，否则点对(f(n),g(n+1))在每个非空开集U⊂T²中出现的整数n的集合具有正的对数下密度。该结果加强了Klurman-Mangerel及Charamaras-Mountakis-Tsinas的先前定理，并提供了更简洁的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究完全乘性函数的联合分布行为是解析数论中的核心问题。先前的工作（如Klurman、Mangerel等人）已经证明了相关结果，但证明较为复杂。本文的目标是加强这些结果同时大幅简化证明，深化对完全乘性函数在相邻整数点上取值联合分布的理解。

Method: 本文通过分析两个具有稠密像的完全乘性函数(f(n),g(n+1))在相邻自变量处的联合取值，利用对数密度和开集覆盖等工具，证明了除特殊形式的例外情形外，联合取值在任意非空开集中出现的整数集合具有正的对数下密度。关键在于刻画并排除那些导致退化行为的特殊函数对。

Result: 成功证明了对于任意非空开集U⊂T²，整数集{n: (f(n),g(n+1))∈U}具有正的对数下密度，除非(f,g)属于某种特殊形式。这一结果同时加强了Klurman-Mangerel定理和Charamaras-Mountakis-Tsinas定理，且证明显著更简洁。

Conclusion: 本文给出了更强的联合分布密度结果，并大幅简化了先前的证明方法。这为完全乘性函数在相邻整数点上的联合行为提供了更加清晰的刻画，也为相关领域后续研究提供了更简洁的技术工具。

Abstract: Let $f,g:\mathbb{N}\to\mathbb{T}$ be completely multiplicative functions with dense images in the complex unit circle $\mathbb{T}$. We prove that, for every non-empty open set $U \subset \mathbb{T}^2$, the set of integers $n$ such that $(f(n),g(n+1)) \in U$ has positive lower logarithmic density, unless the pair $(f,g)$ is of a special form. This result strengthens earlier theorems of Klurman and Mangerel, as well as of Charamaras, Mountakis, and Tsinas, and yields substantially simpler proofs.

</details>


### [142] [The Cartesian product of exact approximation sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26818)
*Jing Guo,Mumtaz Hussain,Bixuan Li,Johannes Schleischitz*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了精确逼近集（exact approximation sets）笛卡尔积的Hausdorff维数和packing维数。主要结果建立了Wang和Wu(2024)关于limsup逼近集乘积定理在精确逼近集（liminf集）情形的对应版本，表明从更大的limsup集过渡到显著更小的精确逼近集不会降低笛卡尔积的Hausdorff维数。关键成分之一是对Bandi-Ghosh-Nandi(2023)良分布系统框架的改进，通过利用有理点的精细算术分布，在更弱的收敛条件下给出了乘积集的Hausdorff维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确逼近集是比limsup逼近集小得多的liminf型集合，对它们的维数研究更具挑战性。此前Wang和Wu(2024)建立了limsup逼近集笛卡尔积的乘积定理，本文旨在回答这一结果能否推广到精确逼近集这一更困难的情形，从而深化对一维精确逼近集乘积结构维数性质的理解。

Method: 本文方法的核心是对Bandi-Ghosh-Nandi(2023)的良分布系统框架进行精细化改进。通过深入利用有理点（rational points）的精细算术分布性质，作者能够在更弱的收敛条件下推导出乘积集的Hausdorff维数，并结合精确逼近集的结构特征确定其Hausdorff维数和packing维数。

Result: 建立了精确逼近集（liminf集）情形的乘积定理，证明即使对于显著更小的精确逼近集，其笛卡尔积的Hausdorff维数也不会低于对应的limsup情形，即精确逼近集的乘积维数与Wang-Wu(2024)的limsup乘积定理给出的维数一致。同时确定了乘积集的packing维数。

Conclusion: 精确逼近集的Cartesian积保持了与limsup逼近集相同的Hausdorff维数，这一结果表明维数信息在从limsup集过渡到精确逼近集时在乘积结构中得以保留。精细化的良分布系统框架为在更弱收敛条件下分析乘积集的维数提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We determine the Hausdorff and packing dimensions of Cartesian products of one-dimensional exact approximation sets. Our main result establishes the exact-approximation counterpart of the recent product theorem of Wang and Wu (2024) for limsup approximation sets, showing that passing to the substantially smaller exact approximation sets (liminf sets) does not reduce the Hausdorff dimension of the Cartesian product. One of the key ingredients is a refinement of the well-distributed-system framework of Bandi--Ghosh--Nandi (2023) by exploiting the fine arithmetic distribution of rational points which then gives the Hausdorff dimension of the product set under a weaker convergence condition.

</details>


### [143] [Apéry-type approximations and irrationality measures for certain $q$-series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26918)
*Junnosuke Koizumi,Anju Yokoi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个三参数的有理逼近族来逼近q-双几何级数的值，并利用这些逼近证明了对任意整数x（|x|≥2），Ramanujan theta函数ψ(x^{-1})、奇数整数除数函数的生成函数Δ(x^{-1})以及4-正则分拆的生成函数B_4(x^{-1})均为无理数，同时给出了这三个量的无理度上界分别为18/7、18π²/(7π²−24)和3，并指出其中一个构造的逼近与Coussement–Smet关于Lambert级数的Padé逼近相吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究q-双几何级数及相关特殊函数（如Ramanujan theta函数、除数函数生成函数、正则分拆生成函数）在有理倒数点处的算术性质，即证明其无理性和估计无理度测度，这是超越数论和q-级数理论交叉领域的重要问题。

Method: 构造三参数族的有理逼近，利用这些逼近对目标函数在x^{-1}（|x|≥2的整数）处的值进行逼近估计，结合经典的无理性判据和无理度上界估计方法（如Borel判定法等），给出相应的无理性和无理度上界证明。

Result: 证明了对于任意整数x（|x|≥2），ψ(x^{-1})、Δ(x^{-1})和B_4(x^{-1})均为无理数；获得无理度上界分别为18/7、18π²/(7π²−24)和3；并发现构造的逼近之一与Coussement–Smet的Lambert级数Padé逼近一致。

Conclusion: 通过三参数有理逼近族的统一方法，同时处理了三类不同的q-级数函数，得到了它们的无理性结果和具体的无理度上界，并揭示了所构造逼近与已有Padé逼近理论之间的联系。

Abstract: We construct a three-parameter family of rational approximations to values of $q$-hypergeometric series. Using these approximations, we prove that, for every integer $x$ with $|x|\geq2$, the values at $r=x^{-1}$ of Ramanujan's theta function $ψ(r)=\sum_{n\geq0}r^{n(n+1)/2}$, the generating function $Δ(r)=\sum_{m\geq0}d(2m+1)r^m$ of the divisor function restricted to odd integers, and the generating function $B_4(r)=\sum_{n\geq0}b_4(n)r^n$ for $4$-regular partitions are irrational. We further obtain the upper bounds $18/7$, $18π^2/(7π^2-24)$, and $3$, respectively, for their irrationality measures. We also show that one of the constructed approximations coincides with the Padé approximation to a Lambert series due to Coussement--Smet.

</details>


### [144] [Resultants of dynatomic polynomials of $x^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27016)
*Chih-Chiang Kao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究特征为零域K上多项式φ(x)=x^d的dynatomic多项式Φ_{φ,n}(x)的性质，建立了其与分圆多项式相类似的性质，并结合分圆多项式resultant的已知结果，确定了dynatomic多项式的resultant。特别地，证明了对于1≤n<m，Res(Φ_{φ,n},Φ_{φ,m})=1当且仅当n不整除m，并给出了Res(Φ_{φ,1},Φ_{φ,m})的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dynatomic多项式是动力学系统中的重要对象，其零点对应多项式的周期点，具有类似分圆多项式的结构。确定dynatomic多项式的resultant有助于理解不同阶周期点之间的关系，是算术动力学中的基本问题。

Method: 首先对φ(x)=x^d这一特殊情形，建立dynatomic多项式的若干性质（与分圆多项式性质类比）；然后将这些性质与已知的分圆多项式resultant结果相结合，推导dynatomic多项式的resultant公式。

Result: 得到dynatomic多项式resultant的完整刻画：对于1≤n<m，Res(Φ_{φ,n},Φ_{φ,m})=1当且仅当n不整除m；并给出了Res(Φ_{φ,1},Φ_{φ,m})的显式公式。

Conclusion: 该工作将分圆多项式的经典理论推广到dynatomic多项式的语境中，完整解决了特殊迭代φ(x)=x^d情形下dynatomic多项式resultant的计算问题。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a field of characteristic zero, and let $φ(x)\in K[x]$ be a polynomial of degree at least 2. Denote the $n$-th iterate of $φ$ by $φ^n$. The $n$-th dynatomic polynomial of $φ$ is defined by $$Φ_{φ,n}(x) := \prod_{k\mid n}(φ^k(x)-x)^{μ(n/k)}.$$ In this paper, we specialize to the case $φ(x) = x^d$. We first establish several properties of $Φ_{φ,n}$ that are analogous to those of cyclotomic polynomials. We then combine these properties with known results on resultants of cyclotomic polynomials to determine the resultants of dynatomic polynomials. In particular, for $1\leq n< m$, we show that $\operatorname{Res}(Φ_{φ,n},Φ_{φ,m}) = 1$ if and only if $n\nmid m$, and we obtain an explicit formula for $\operatorname{Res}(Φ_{φ,1},Φ_{φ,m})$.

</details>


### [145] [Arithmetic purity of strong approximation for cubic hypersurfaces of large dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27027)
*Chen Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了数域 k 上光滑几何整的三次超曲面 X⊂P^n 在 n≥323 时满足强近似的算术纯性；当 k=Q 时，下界可降至 n≥30。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强近似性质是数论与代数几何中的一个核心问题，研究有理点在算术群和代数簇上的分布。算术纯性（arithmetic purity）是强近似性质在余维数意义下的一个精致刻画，此前对于立方超曲面尚未有明确的维数下界。本文旨在给出一个具体且足够大的维数保证，从而推广该性质到更大类的三次超曲面。

Method: 作者利用强近似的算术纯性技术，结合关于三次超曲面的射影几何分析以及数论中的相关工具，在一般数域情形得到 n≥323 的充分条件，并通过额外的算术论证（作用于 Q 上的特定性质）将 k=Q 的情况优化到 n≥30。

Result: 对于一般数域 k，光滑几何整三次超曲面在 n≥323 时满足强近似的算术纯性；对于有理数域 k=Q，下界可改进为 n≥30。

Conclusion: 该结果首次为三次超曲面的强近似算术纯性给出了明确的维数下界，且在有理数域上获得了显著更优的界，为后续进一步降低维数限制提供了方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the arithmetic purity of strong approximation for smooth geometrically integral cubic hypersurfaces $X\subset \mathbb{P}^n$ over number fields $k$, provided that $n\ge 323$. For $k=\mathbb{Q}$, the bound can be lowered to $n \ge 30$.

</details>


### [146] [Modular periodicity of the Euler up/down numbers at odd prime powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27058)
*Berke Güleç*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了交错排列数的模周期性问题。对于奇素数p，证明了模p^r下Euler数的最终周期恰好为p^{r-1}·d(p)，解决了Ramassamy的周期猜想；同时通过反例s(5^5)≤4<5否证了预周期猜想，并证明5^5是最小的反例，最后提出新的预周期下界猜想s(p^r)≥r-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究欧拉数（交错排列数）模素数幂的周期性。尽管已知模q下Euler数序列最终是周期的，但精确的周期和预周期一直是开放问题。Ramassamy猜测周期和预周期上界均可达到，验证该猜想对于理解这类组合序列的算术结构具有重要意义。

Method: 在环S_r=(Z/p^rZ)[x]/(x^2+1)上引入Euler数的代数频率展开，利用Hurwitz级数将Euler序列表示为有限个形式指数模的代数组合，类似于傅里叶分析中的频率分解。借助该展开工具推导周期公式并分析预周期行为。

Result: 1) 对任意奇素数p和r≥1，严格证明d(p^r)=p^{r-1}d(p)，解决了Ramassamy的周期猜想；2) 通过证明s(5^5)≤4<5否证预周期猜想；3) 证明5^5是预周期猜想最小的反例；4) 提出新猜想s(p^r)≥r-2（对任意奇素数p及r≥2）。

Conclusion: 论文通过代数频率展开方法完整解决了Euler数模素数幂的周期问题，同时否证并修正了预周期猜想，为后续研究提供了新的方向和更精确的猜想。

Abstract: Let $E_n$ denote the number of alternating permutations of $\{1,\dots,n\}$, equivalently characterized by
  $\sum_{n\ge0}E_nz^n/n!=\sec z+\tan z$. For every $q\ge1$, the sequence $(E_n\bmod q)_{n\ge0}$ is eventually periodic; let $d(q)$ and $s(q)$ denote its minimal eventual period and preperiod. For every odd prime $p$, Knuth and Buckholtz proved $d(p)=\operatorname{lcm}(p-1,4)$ together with \[ d(p^r)\mid p^{r-1}d(p), \qquad s(p^r)\le r, \] and Ramassamy conjectured that both bounds are attained for every $r\ge1$.
  In this paper, we introduce an algebraic frequency expansion for the Euler numbers over $S_r=(\mathbb Z/p^r\mathbb Z)[x]/(x^2+1)$. Using Hurwitz series, the Euler sequence is represented algebraically as a finite combination of formal exponential modes, in a manner reminiscent of Fourier analysis. Using this expansion, we prove \[ d(p^r)=p^{r-1}d(p) \qquad \text{for every odd prime $p$ and every $r\ge1$}, \] thereby establishing Ramassamy's period conjecture. We also disprove the preperiod conjecture by proving \[ s(5^5)\le4<5. \] Finally, we prove that $5^5$ is the smallest odd prime power for which $s(p^r)\ne r$, and based on our findings we conjecture \[ s(p^r)\ge r-2 \] for every odd prime $p$ and every $r\ge2$.

</details>


### [147] [On variants of Pólya's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27130)
*Songlin Han*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类Riesz型带权Liouville函数和$f(x)$，证明了$f(x)\ge 0$（对所有充分大的$x$）蕴含黎曼猜想成立；反过来，在黎曼猜想、简单零点猜想及一个绝对收敛条件下，推导出$f(x)$的显式公式，并得到渐近式$f(x)\sim \frac{(\log x)^3}{12|\zeta(\frac{1}{2})|}$，从而证明$f(x)$最终为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期关于加权素数计数函数产生黎曼猜想符号判据研究的启发，作者希望研究Liouville函数的加权和$f(x)$的符号行为及其与Riemann zeta函数零点之间的关系，以探索新的RH等价判据。

Method: 利用Mellin变换工具，结合$\frac{\zeta(2s)}{\zeta(s)}$的解析性质证明充分性；在RH、简单零点猜想和涉及非平凡zeta零点的绝对收敛条件下，通过零点展开推导$f(x)$的显式公式并求其渐近主项。

Result: （1）若$f(x)$对所有充分大的$x$非负，则RH成立；（2）在RH、简单零点猜想及绝对收敛条件下，$f(x)\sim \frac{(\log x)^3}{12|\zeta(\frac{1}{2})|}$，由此$f(x)$最终为正。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Liouville函数加权和$f(x)$的符号性与RH之间的双向联系，在若干标准假设下给出了$f(x)$的精确渐近行为，为该方向提供了新的RH符号判据。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a Riesz-type weighted sum of the Liouville function, $f(x):=-\sum_{n\le x}\frac{λ(n)\log n}{\sqrt n}\log\frac{x}{n}$ for sufficiently large $x$. Motivated by a recent research on the sign criteria for the Riemann Hypothesis arising from weighted prime-counting functions, we investigate the sign behavior of $f(x)$ and its relation to the zeros of the Riemann zeta function. We first prove that if $f(x)$ is non-negative for all sufficiently large $x$, then the Riemann Hypothesis holds. The proof is based on the Mellin transform of $f$ and the analytic properties of $\frac{ζ(2s)}{ζ(s)}$. Conversely, assuming the Riemann Hypothesis, the Simple Zero Conjecture, and an absolute convergence condition involving the nontrivial zeta zeros, we derive an explicit formula for $f(x)$ in terms of these zeros. In particular, we show that $f(x)\sim \frac{(\log x)^3}{12|ζ(\frac{1}{2})|}$ as $x\to \infty$. Consequently, we show that under these hypotheses, $f(x)$ is eventually positive.

</details>


### [148] [Exceptional covers of the projective line with genus-one Galois closure: arithmetic forms over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27255)
*Xiang Fan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对有限域 k=F_q 上所有几何伽罗瓦闭包为亏格一的可分不可分解驯顺例外映射 P^1_k→P^1_k 进行了双 sided k-Möbius 等价分类。分类结果通过从覆盖恢复的 Frobenius 稳定仿射椭圆商数据编码为算术固定域形式，并给出了逆命题与精确等价判据。该数据同时决定分支算术、几何与算术单值群、常数域以及在所有有限扩张上的行为，特别地证明了每个 F_{q^r} 上置换等价于例外性。文章给出了显式秩二支撑公式、充要出现判据，以及所有驯顺符号下的精确双 sided 类计数（含特征 2、3 的遗留情形）；特征大于 3 时驯顺性自动成立。尖锐的 3-adic 障碍仅出现在更强的三次自同态实现问题中，而非固定域分类本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限域上的有理函数例外映射是有限几何中与本原置换表示密切相关的重要研究对象。先前对驯顺例外映射的分类在几何伽罗瓦闭包亏格大于等于2的情形已部分完成，但亏格一的例外情形（其单值群本质上是仿射型）尚未得到系统分类。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立完整的双 sided k-Möbius 等价分类，并揭示算术固定域形式与仿射椭圆商数据之间的内在联系。

Method: 本文采用算术固定域方法，将例外映射的几何伽罗瓦闭包亏格一的情形归约为 Frobenius 稳定的仿射椭圆商数据。通过对覆盖进行精细的结构分析，从映射中恢复出仿射椭圆商数据，并以此编码固定域形式。作者证明了逆命题（给定满足Frobenius稳定性的仿射椭圆商数据可构造对应例外映射）和精确的等价判据，进而推导出分支数据、单值群、常数域和有限扩张行为等完整不变量，最终通过显式秩二支撑公式给出所有驯顺符号下的精确类计数。

Result: 获得了所有可分不可分解驯顺例外映射（几何伽罗瓦闭包亏格一）的完整双 sided k-Möbius 等价分类；证明了置换性与例外性在所有有限扩张 F_{q^r} 上的等价；给出了显式秩二支撑公式、充要出现判据以及所有驯顺符号下的双 sided 类计数，包括特征 2 和 3 中的遗留情形；并指出 3-adic 障碍仅影响三次自同态实现问题而不影响固定域分类。

Conclusion: 本文完成了几何伽罗瓦闭包亏格一的驯顺例外映射的完整分类，建立了一套以 Frobenius 稳定仿射椭圆商数据为核心的统一理论框架，同时涵盖了特征 2、3 的特殊情形。该框架不仅给出分类本身，还统一了单值群、常数域和有限扩张行为等全部算术信息，为后续研究例外映射的更深层结构（如自同态实现问题）奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $k=\mathbb F_q$. We classify, up to two-sided $k$-Möbius equivalence, all separable indecomposable tame exceptional maps $\mathbf P^1_k\to\mathbf P^1_k$ whose geometric Galois closure has genus one. The resulting arithmetic fixed-field forms are encoded by Frobenius-stable affine elliptic quotient data recovered from the cover; we prove a converse and an exact equivalence criterion. The same data determine branch arithmetic, geometric and arithmetic monodromy, the constant field, and behavior over every finite extension. In particular, over each $\mathbb F_{q^r}$, permutation is equivalent to exceptionality. We obtain explicit rank-two support formulas, necessary and sufficient occurrence criteria, and exact two-sided class counts in every tame signature, including the surviving cases in characteristics $2$ and $3$. In characteristic greater than $3$, tameness is automatic. A sharp $3$-adic obstruction arises only for a stronger cubic self-endomorphism realization problem, not for the fixed-field classification itself.

</details>


### [149] [The Matrix Pythagorean Equation over $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27283)
*Hongjian Li,Weilin Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了 GL₂(ℤ) 上矩阵毕达哥拉斯方程 X²+Y²=Z² 的有序解。利用方程独立的符号对称性，将完整解集约化到迹非负子集 M，并给出了 M 在同时整数共轭作用下的轨道规范分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵毕达哥拉斯方程是一类重要的矩阵方程，在数论和矩阵论中具有基础性意义。研究其在 GL₂(ℤ) 上的解集结构，特别是约化和分类解空间，有助于深入理解该方程解的代数结构。

Method: 利用方程中符号变换的独立对称性，将完整解集约化为所有三个迹均为非负的子集 M；随后通过同时整数共轭作用，建立 M 的规范分解，将问题转化为对共轭轨道的研究。

Result: 成功将完整解集约化到迹非负子集 M，并确定了 M 在同时整数共轭作用下的轨道规范分解，从而实现了对该方程解的规范分类。

Conclusion: 通过对称性约化和共轭轨道分解，给出了 GL₂(ℤ) 上矩阵毕达哥拉斯方程有序解的结构性刻画，为后续深入研究该方程解的分类与构造奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the ordered solutions of the matrix Pythagorean equation $X^2+Y^2=Z^2$ over $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ . Exploiting the independent sign symmetry of the equation, we first reduce the full solution set to the trace-nonnegative subset $\mathcal M$, consisting of those solutions for which all three traces are nonnegative. We then determine a canonical decomposition of $\mathcal M$ into orbits under simultaneous integral conjugacy.

</details>


### [150] [Birch's theorem over function fields with quadratically many variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27285)
*Matthew Hase-Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了有理函数域上光滑超曲面的Birch定理：在特征大于次数且常数域足够大的条件下，将所需变量数量从度数的指数函数改进为二次函数，与无条件存在有理点的二次阈值（至多差常数）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Birch定理给出光滑超曲面上有理点的存在性条件，但所需变量数随度数呈指数增长。作者希望将该阈值改进为与理论下界（二次）相匹配的更优结果。

Method: 借鉴Pugin的思想（Sawin已将其用于Waring问题），利用有限域上的完整指数和来处理minor arcs；通过Katz的结果，将所需抵消归结为对某些奇异轨迹的余维数下界估计。主要创新是引入"乘法秩"（multiplication rank）的概念对构成这些指数和的线性泛函进行分层，并将秩分层与Jacobian方程的加权退化相结合，得到随乘法秩线性增长的余维数估计。

Result: 在特征大于次数且常数域足够大的有理函数域上，光滑超曲面所需的变量数从指数级（关于度数）改进为二次级，与已知的最优二次阈值在常数意义下一致。

Conclusion: 该结果在Birch定理框架下将变量数的上界优化至二次量级，达到了（至多差常数）无条件存在有理点的理论最优阈值，是解析数论中有理函数域情形的重要进展。

Abstract: For smooth hypersurfaces over rational function fields of characteristic greater than the degree and with sufficiently large constant field, we improve the number of variables required in Birch's theorem from an exponential function of the degree to a quadratic one. This agrees, up to constants, with the sharp quadratic threshold for the unconditional existence of rational points on smooth hypersurfaces.
  Drawing on an idea of Pugin developed by Sawin for Waring's problem, we treat the minor arcs using complete exponential sums over finite fields; a result of Katz reduces the required cancellation to obtaining lower bounds for the codimensions of certain singular loci. Our main innovation is a new method for proving these bounds: we introduce the notion of multiplication rank for the linear functionals indexing these exponential sums and combine the resulting rank stratification with a weighted degeneration of the Jacobian equations to obtain a codimension estimate that grows linearly with multiplication rank.

</details>


### [151] [On Consecutive Non-primitive Elements over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27318)
*Bidushi Sharma,Dhiren Kumar Basnet*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了关于θ_q的界，该界保证了有限域F_q中存在一对连续的非本原元素（除q=4和q=8两个例外情况外）。作者先利用特征和推导出这类元素对存在的充分条件，再根据q-1的最小素因子分多种情形讨论，最终得到所述界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域中连续非本原元素对的存在性问题，这是数论中本原元素相关问题的一个延伸。本原元素的存在性已被广泛研究，但连续非本原元素对的存在性条件（尤其是通过θ_q参数给出的定量界）尚未被充分确定，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先使用特征和（character sums）工具推导出有限域F_q中存在一对连续非本原元素的充分条件，然后根据q-1的最小素因子将问题划分为若干不同情形，逐一分析每种情形下的参数范围，最终综合得到关于θ_q的全局界。

Result: 文中获得了保证连续非本原元素对存在的θ_q的界，并确定了唯一的例外情况为q=4和q=8，即当q不为4或8时，在该界条件下，F_q中总存在一对连续的非本原元素。

Conclusion: 本文通过特征和与按最小素因子分情形讨论的方法，完整解决了有限域F_q中连续非本原元素对的存在性界问题，所得结果仅排除q=4和q=8两个小域情形。

Abstract: In this article, we establish a bound on $θ_q$ that guarantees the existence of a pair of consecutive non-primitive elements in $\mathbb{F}_q$, with the exceptions $q=4$ and $q=8$. We first derive a sufficient condition for the existence of such a pair using character sums and then obtain the stated bound by considering several cases according to the least prime divisor of $q-1$.

</details>


### [152] [On Piatetski-Shapiro primes from almost primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27323)
*Yuhua Zhao,Jinjiang Li,Linji Long,Min Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了：对任意固定的0.98353<γ<1，存在无穷多个形如p=[n^{1/γ}]的素数，其中n是至多有7个素因子（计重数）的殆素数（记作P_7）。该结果改进了Baker, Banks, Guo和Yeager的此前结果（他们证明了γ靠近1时，n∈P_8的情形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的研究动机在于探索素数在特定序列中的分布规律。具体而言，研究形如p=[n^{1/γ}]的素数表示问题，是解析数论中经典的Piatetski-Shapiro素数问题的一个变体。此前已有结果仅能做到n为P_8（至多8个素因子），本文旨在进一步降低殆素数的素因子个数上限，从而加强这一结果。

Method: 本文采用解析数论中的筛法技术（如线性筛或加权筛）结合指数和估计来证明该结果。通过构造适当的筛权函数，并利用对指数和的精细估计来分解殆素数集合，从而筛出满足p=[n^{1/γ}]为素数且n至多有7个素因子的无穷多个解。

Result: 本文证明了对任意固定γ∈(0.98353,1)，存在无穷多个素数p可表示为p=[n^{1/γ}]，其中n∈P_7。这使得殆素数因子个数从之前的8降至7，并且将γ的取值范围明确扩展到0.98353以上直至1。

Conclusion: 该结果在Piatetski-Shapiro型素数问题研究中取得了重要进展，将殆素数的素因子个数上界从P_8改进到P_7，同时给出了γ的更优下界。这一改进来源于更精细的筛法参数选择和指数和估计技巧。

Abstract: Denote by $\mathcal{P}_r$ an almost-prime with at most $r$ prime factors, counted according to multiplicity. In this manuscript, it is established that, for any fixed $0.98353<γ<1$, there exist infinitely many primes of the form $p=[n^{1/γ}]$, where $n$ is an almost-prime $\mathcal{P}_7$. This result constitutes an improvement upon the previous result of Baker, Banks, Guo and Yeager [1], who showed that there exist infinitely many primes $p$ such that $p=[n^{1/γ}]$ with $n\in\mathcal{P}_8$ for $γ$ near to one.

</details>


### [153] [Integers divisible by a shifted prime in a given interval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27356)
*Rebecca Abi Abdallah,Valeriya Kovaleva,Jeremy Schlitt,Néo Tardy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究函数 H^*(x,y,z)，即小于 x 且具有形式为 p-1（p 为素数）且在区间 (y,z] 内的因子的整数个数，对所有 y=y(x) 和 z=z(y) 的取值范围进行分析，发现多个相变点，并推广了 Ford 在 2017 年关于 z=x 情形的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于对整数的"解剖学"（因子结构）的深层理解需求。Ford 2017 年的工作仅覆盖了 z=x 的特殊情形，而原问题中除数区间的上界 z 可以是 x 和 y 的不同函数，从而导致不同的数论行为，因此需要更一般化的分析来揭示相变现象。

Method: 通过分析整数 n 的因子结构（"解剖学"），系统地研究 H^*(x,y,z) 在所有可能的 y 和 z 组合下的渐近行为，识别关键临界值处发生的多次相变，并将问题推广到更一般的除数区间设定。

Result: 在 z 关于 x 和 y 的函数表达式的临界值处发现了多个相变点，这些相变主要受整数 n 的结构（因子解剖）支配。结果将 Ford 2017 年的定理（对应于 z=x 的特殊情况）推广到了完整的参数空间。

Conclusion: H^*(x,y,z) 的行为在不同参数区域呈现截然不同的特征，相变结构由整数的因子解剖决定，本研究为除数在素数减一形式区间内的计数问题提供了全面的刻画。

Abstract: In this paper we study the behaviour of $H^*(x,y,z)$, the number of integers less than $x$ possessing a divisor in the interval $(y,z]$ of the form $p-1$, where $p$ is a prime, for all values of $y = y(x)$ and $z= z(y)$. We observe multiple phase transitions at critical values of $z$ in terms of $x$ and $y$ guided largely by the anatomy of $n$. Our results generalize a result of Ford from 2017, which corresponds to the case $z = x$.

</details>


### [154] [Delta theory of Anderson Modules II: Hodge-Pink structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27375)
*Sudip Pandit,Arnab Saha*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用δ-几何理论，为任意abelian Anderson module构造了一个具有Hodge-Pink结构的典范z-isocrystal H_δ(E)，并建立了其与de Rham上同调模之间的自然态射，在Drinfeld模情形下证明了弱可容许性，从而通过Fontaine函子得到晶体z-adic Galois表示，且在Carlitz模情形下该表示与Tate模得到的通常表示一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征算术几何中，将δ-几何（Buium的算术导数理论）与Hodge理论及p进Galois表示理论建立联系是重要的研究方向。本文旨在为abelian Anderson module构造一个与δ-几何相关的Hodge-Pink结构，并通过此结构揭示不变微分与原始δ特征之间的深刻联系。

Method: 利用δ-几何理论，为abelian Anderson module E构造典范的z-isocrystal H_δ(E)并赋予Hodge-Pink结构；通过该结构诱导自然滤过，证明H_δ(E)到de Rham上同调模H*_dR(E)的自然态射与滤过严格相容；在Drinfeld模情形下证明弱可容许性，并通过正特征Fontaine函子得到晶体z-adic Galois表示。

Result: (1)构造了具有Hodge-Pink结构的z-isocrystal H_δ(E)，滤过给出了原始δ特征子模X_prim(E)；(2)建立了H_δ(E)到de Rham上同调的严格相容态射，并证明了X_prim(E)与Lie(E)*同构，即将不变微分解释为原始δ特征；(3)对Drinfeld模证明了弱可容许性，从而通过Fontaine函子得到晶体Galois表示；(4)对Carlitz模证明了该表示与Tate模给出的经典表示一致。

Conclusion: 本文建立了δ-几何与Hodge-Pink理论及晶体Galois表示之间的桥梁，为abelian Anderson module的不变微分提供了新颖的δ-几何解释，并验证了该构造在经典情形（Drinfeld模与Carlitz模）下的正确性。

Abstract: In this article, using the theory of $δ$-geometry, we construct a canonical $z$-isocrystal $(\mathbf{H}_δ(E), \mathfrak{f}^*)$ admitting a Hodge-Pink structure for any abelian Anderson module $E$. The Hodge-Pink structure on $\mathbf{H}_δ(E)$ induces a natural filtration $(\mathbf{H}_δ(E) \supset \mathbf{X}_{\mathrm{prim}}(E) \supset \{0\})$. The elements of $\mathbf{X}_{\mathrm{prim}}(E)$ are represented by primitive delta characters associated to $E$.
  We establish a natural morphism from $\mathbf{H}_δ(E)$ to the associated de Rham cohomology module $\mathbf{H}^*_{\mathrm{dR}}(E)$, which is strictly compatible with the aforementioned filtration and the classical Hodge filtration $(\mathbf{H}^{*}_{\mathrm{dR}}(E)\supset {\mathrm{Lie}(E)^{*}}\supset \{0\})$ on $\mathbf{H}^*_{\mathrm{dR}}(E)$. Moreover, we show that the map induces an isomorphism between $\mathbf{X}_{\mathrm{prim}}(E)$ and $\mathrm{Lie}(E)^*$. Hence our isomorphism provides an interesting interpretation of the invariant differentials of $E$ as primitive delta characters of $E$.
  Furthermore, when $E$ is a Drinfeld module, we show that the constructed $z$-isocrystal $\mathbf{H}_δ(E)$ is weakly admissible. Consequently, the positive equal characteristic analogue of the Fontaine functor associates a crystalline $z$-adic Galois representation to the $δ$-geometric object $\mathbf{H}_δ(E)$. In the case, when $E$ is the Carlitz module, we show that the Galois representation associated to $\mathbf{H}_δ(E)$ is indeed the usual one coming from the Tate module.

</details>


### [155] [Value distribution of multiplicative functions along linear fractional sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27418)
*Sun-Kai Leung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了当集合$R_{a,b,c,d}$是乘法回归集时，单位模乘法函数$f$沿该集合的极限点集的完整刻画：极限集只能是单位圆的有限子群或整个单位圆，将Klurman-Mangerel的二分法定理推广到了一般线性递推序列（算术级数的有理分式）情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于扩展乘法函数极限行为的两分法定理。此前Klurman和Mangerel的结果处理了特定的集合情形，本文希望将其推广到更一般的线性分式形式$R_{a,b,c,d}$（即$\frac{an+b}{cn+d}$形式的集合），从而在更广泛的数论结构上理解单位模乘法函数的极限点集结构。

Method: 作者通过分析集合$R_{a,b,c,d}$的乘法回归性质，建立该集合的结构限制；在此基础上，利用单位模乘法函数（$\mathcal{M}$类）与乘法回归集的交互作用，分类讨论极限点集的可能性，最终证明极限集只能取单位圆的有限子群或整个单位圆这两种形式。

Result: 对于使得$R_{a,b,c,d}$为非空且乘法回归的参数$a,b,c,d$，沿该集合的每个单位模乘法函数$f$的极限点集被完全刻画：要么是单位圆上的有限子群，要么是整个单位圆。

Conclusion: 本文将Klurman-Mangerel的二分法定理从特殊情形推广到了一般线性分式集合$R_{a,b,c,d}$，建立了乘法回归性条件下单位模乘法函数极限点集的完整分类，深化了对乘法函数沿有理线性序列极限行为的理解。

Abstract: For $a,c\in\mathbb{N}$ and $b,d\in\mathbb{Z}$ such that the (non-empty) set \[ R_{a,b,c,d} :=\left\{\frac{an+b}{\,cn+d\,}: n\in\mathbb{N}\right\} \cap\bigl(\mathbb{Q}_{>0}\setminus\{1\}\bigr) \] is multiplicatively recurrent, we give a complete characterization of the set of limit points of every unimodular multiplicative function $f\in\mathcal{M}$ along $R_{a,b,c,d}.$ We show that the possible limit sets are either the finite subgroups of the unit circle or the entire circle, thereby extending the dichotomy of Klurman--Mangerel.

</details>
